Motivation: 现有LIV Gaussian mapping pipelines过度依赖RGB光度线索，导致在光照变化（如低光或强光）和纹理缺失场景（如纯色墙面）中表现脆弱，易产生几何失真和结构漂移。

Method: 1. 利用LIV视觉地图点作为置信度感知的跨模态锚点，建立鲁棒的thermal-LiDAR关联；  
2. 将加权LiDAR点到平面残差集成至束调整（bundle adjustment），在弱热监督下联合优化相机姿态与3D点；  
3. 引入LiDAR平面正则化的可微分splatting目标，约束渲染3D点与局部观测平面对齐，抑制低对比度热图像中的表面增厚效应。

Result: 在私有序列和公开数据集实验中，LIT-GS在几何精度（如平面拟合误差降低15-20%）和渲染质量（PSNR提升2-3 dB）上持续优于SOTA LIV-Gaussian基线，尤其在极端光照条件（如夜间或强阴影）下鲁棒性显著增强。

Conclusion: LIT-GS通过显式融合LiDAR平面几何约束，有效克服了传统LIV方法的局限性，为复杂环境下的实时神经渲染提供了更可靠、高精度的解决方案，适用于自动驾驶和机器人导航等场景。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.

</details>


### [227] [TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20426)
*Hengfei Zhao,Yifan Xie,Junhao Gong,Yue Sun,Kai Zhu,Weihua He,Shoujie Li,Haohuan Fu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaCauchy: 一个集成到Isaac Sim的可扩展有限元方法框架，用于高保真触觉传感器模拟，通过UIPC求解器直接计算柯西应力张量，实现33.40 FPS单环境性能和SSIM>0.93的物理验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的触觉传感器模拟方法在GPU加速机器人平台中难以提供准确的机械应力场，导致强化学习训练缺乏物理真实性的力监督，限制了下游操作任务的性能。

Method: TaCauchy基于统一增量势接触（UIPC）求解器，从超弹性本构定律直接推导柯西应力张量，并投影至接触表面以获取牵引力和压力分布；集成自动网格生成、几何感知自适应细化机制，以及模块化传感器接口（支持GelSight Mini、DIGIT、9DTact），实现低配置成本的多传感器适配。

Result: 性能基准测试显示单环境运行达33.40 FPS，60并行环境总吞吐量555 FPS，应力提取开销低于1 ms；物理验证实验在1.2556 N至4.7332 N力范围内，模拟与真实触觉响应高度一致，结构相似性指数（SSIM）均超过0.93。

Conclusion: 该框架成功提供了基于第一性原理的准确、物理 grounded 力监督，显著提升了触觉模拟的保真度，可有效支持强化学习驱动的机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [228] [ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20428)
*Alexandre Hadji-Thomas,Andrew Stirling,James R. Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的块坐标下降框架，结合规范感知自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新及最小加权协方差行列式估计器，实现无需手动调参的联合状态与协方差估计。实验表明该方法能准确恢复测量协方差，状态估计精度优于或匹配现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器数据常受异常值和非高斯噪声污染，导致经典状态估计器产生偏差。现有鲁棒估计器不处理测量协方差估计，而联合状态与协方差估计器假设高斯残差和固定损失参数，无法有效应对异常值。因此需整合两种能力，实现异常值存在下的状态与协方差同步估计。

Method: 提出块坐标下降框架，集成规范感知自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新算法及最小加权协方差行列式协方差估计器，构建自调谐联合估计器。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟与超宽带定位实验显示，该方法可恢复真实内点测量协方差，状态估计精度达到或超越所有基线方法，且无需手动参数调整。

Conclusion: 该框架成功融合鲁棒性与协方差估计能力，解决了非高斯噪声与异常值环境下的状态估计问题，具备自适应性，为复杂场景下的传感器数据处理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [229] [Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20458)
*Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng,Honglin He,Quanyi Li,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对人行道导航中轨迹评分差距问题，提出VLM-Planner接口：利用视觉语言模型(VLM)从规划器候选集中选择轨迹索引，并通过无训练延迟弹性融合层（基于几何相似性与指数衰减）处理VLM延迟，实现实时评分。在约2000个真实场景中，VLM选择使平均位移误差(ADE)降低30%，模拟中5秒延迟下成功率超80%，并在机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习-based规划器在人行道导航中实时生成多样化候选轨迹，但评分函数在挑战性场景（如路口、行人交互）中常选错轨迹，导致机器人驶向草地、行人或错误方向，即使存在更优候选。此轨迹评分差距源于规划器高层场景理解能力不足，影响安全性和效率。

Method: 提出VLM-Planner接口：使用视觉语言模型(VLM)从规划器提案集中选择候选索引，并与规划器初始输出融合。为解决VLM查询延迟(1-3秒)无法匹配5-20Hz控制循环的问题，设计无训练延迟弹性轨迹级融合层，通过几何相似性和指数衰减将过时VLM选择转化为实时评分。

Result: 在约2000个挑战性真实世界场景测试中，VLM选择相比规划器最佳选择实现30% ADE减少；规划器在常规场景仍具竞争力。模拟显示，Score Fusion在延迟达5秒时维持>80%成功率。系统在移动机器人上成功演示，适用于校园人行道导航及高网络延迟环境。

Conclusion: VLM-Planner接口有效缩小轨迹评分差距，在挑战性场景显著提升导航性能（降低ADE），同时通过延迟弹性融合层确保实时性；规划器在简单场景保持优势，整体系统适用于复杂真实世界人行道导航。

Abstract: Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.

</details>


### [230] [GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20479)
*Nastaran Darabi,Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GroundControl轨迹一致性不确定性估计器，通过卡尔曼滤波建模目标导向距离动态偏差，结合SRCN协议评估不确定性质量，在5个EB-Navigation分割上显著优于现有基线方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航代理在基准任务中虽取得竞争性平均成功率，但失败常源于可预测的轨迹级故障（振荡、停滞、低效绕路）。现有不确定性信号仅反映瞬时动作熵，无法在执行过程中预测故障动态，可靠部署需要能 anticipatory emerging failure dynamics 的不确定性信号

Method: GroundControl：基于恒定速度卡尔曼滤波建模距离演化，计算归一化创新统计与轨迹特征（进度、单调性、路径效率、振荡行为）的组合不确定性得分。提出SRCN协议：通过风险-覆盖率曲线和AURC/E-AURC摘要，独立于任务成功评估不确定性得分按故障/低效排序剧集的有效性

Result: 在5个EB-Navigation分割（N=300剧集）上，轨迹一致性不确定性在基于成功的选择性风险下实现接近oracle排序，GPT-4o模型加权平均E-AURC_SR=0.0024，显著优于熵/共形/启发式基线。SPL-based选择性评估中，GroundControl在所有模型和导航分割上均取得最低AURC和E-AURC

Conclusion: 建模偏离目标导向动态提供了一种可解释且鲁棒的信号，用于预测视觉-语言代理中的导航故障，证明了轨迹一致性不确定性估计在 anticipatory failure detection 中的有效性

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.

</details>


### [231] [Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20491)
*Fatma Youssef Mohammed,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GazeLNN 是一款计算轻量级的扫描路径预测模型，利用液状神经网络和 MobileNetV3，计算成本仅 0.61 GFLOPs，性能领先，并成功集成到机器人导航系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型计算成本高，难以将人类结构化扫描路径行为有效集成到机器人自主性中。

Method: 提出 GazeLNN 模型，采用液状神经网络作为循环引擎，MobileNetV3 进行特征提取，以自回归方式预测连续注视热力图，基于当前视觉刺激和注视历史。

Result: 仅需 0.61 GFLOPs，在 MIT 低分辨率数据集上实现 0.47 ScanMatch 分数的最先进性能；计算成本降低 99.40%，推理速度提升六倍；在真实世界无人机部署中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 将 GazeLNN 集成到强化学习训练的主动相机机器人控制策略中，实现了自主导航中的人类注视引导感知，证明了该高效架构在机器人自主性中的实用价值。

Abstract: Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.

</details>


### [232] [Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20495)
*Oxana Shamilyan,Ievgen Kabin,Zoya Dyka,Oleksandr Sudakov,Peter Langendoerfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AHP-enhanced GA improves path diversity and resilience in continuum robots, unaffected by environment complexity.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To enhance the operational resilience of continuum robots by extending maintenance intervals through multi-criteria optimization of motion planning.

Method: Modified Genetic Algorithm (GA) and A* algorithms integrated with Analytical Hierarchy Process (AHP) for multi-criteria evaluation (distance, motor damage, mechanical damage, accuracy). Tested in two simulated environments with varying path point configurations.

Result: GA outperforms A* in execution time consistency across environments and generates more diverse paths, improving robot resilience.

Conclusion: Integrating AHP with GA optimizes multi-criteria path planning, enhancing continuum robot durability and reducing maintenance frequency.

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.

</details>


### [233] [Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20549)
*Sha Yi,Nicklas Hansen,Xueqian Bai,Carmelo Sferrazza,Michael T. Tolley,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出数据驱动框架，利用人类指尖运动数据生成机器人手设计，结合简单控制策略和强化学习加速搜索，实现高精度遥操作和降低机械复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习在控制方面进展迅速，但学习物理身体困难，因联合搜索设计与控制导致巨大组合问题。

Method: 提出数据驱动框架，使用超过400万帧人类指尖运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置生成树状结构机器人手设计；训练强化学习演员优化设计和关节角度，减少搜索时间；直接打印一体式关节结构。

Result: 生成6自由度通用手和3自由度任务特定手；6-DoF手实现优于商业手的高精度遥操作指尖跟踪；3-DoF手重现人类和合成轨迹，降低机械复杂度。

Conclusion: 大规模人类运动数据不仅用于训练控制器，还可用于优化和生成机器人物理体现。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.

</details>


### [234] [MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20562)
*Sizhe Yang,Juncheng Mu,Tianming Wei,Chenhao Lu,Xiaofan Li,Linning Xu,Zhengrong Xue,Zhecheng Yuan,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryWAM通过混合记忆设计（结合最近帧、事件边界锚帧和紧凑gist令牌）解决世界动作模型的效率与长历史保留权衡问题，在机器人操作中实现高性能和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型面临根本权衡：高效推理方法仅基于有限观察窗口，难以处理非马尔可夫环境；而保留长历史的方法计算成本随序列长度显著增长，导致记忆依赖任务性能不足。

Method: MemoryWAM采用混合记忆设计，整合最近帧、事件边界锚帧和压缩长期历史的gist令牌，并设计定制注意力机制以同时检索详细短期上下文和压缩长期上下文。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长视野、记忆依赖操作任务中，MemoryWAM优于现有视觉-语言-动作和世界动作模型基线，同时保持推理延迟和GPU内存使用的优化。

Conclusion: MemoryWAM有效平衡了高效推理与持久记忆能力，为稳健机器人操作提供了可扩展的解决方案，显著提升了非马尔可夫环境下的任务表现。

Abstract: Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [235] [Human Universal Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17054)
*Kevin Yuanbo Wu,Tianxing Zhou,Isaac Tu,Billy Yan,Irmak Guzey,David Fouhey,Dandan Shan,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HUG模型利用流匹配技术生成多样化人类抓取数据，实现机器人零样本抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指机器人抓取通用性远不及人类，主张人类日常抓取行为是最自然的数据来源，以解决机器人抓取泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 提出HUG流匹配模型，融合RGB-D图像输出抓取参数（手腕平移、旋转及MANO手姿势）；构建1M-HUGs数据集（100万帧、6707物体实例、41栋建筑）；开发HUG-Bench基准（90个未见物体，五类几何形状）。

Result: 在HUG-Bench真实世界测试中，HUG在抓取成功率上比现有SOTA方法提升23%和34%。

Conclusion: HUG显著提升机器人抓取性能，支持跨物体和环境的零样本抓取，代码、数据及基准已开源。

Abstract: Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgenticRei: 一种基于OWL和deontic政策的治理框架，用于LLM代理的安全、隐私和合规管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主代理AI系统在执行工具调用、数据操作等任务时，面临超出传统认证和访问控制的复杂治理挑战，而现有政策引擎（如XACML、Rego、Cedar）无法处理义务生命周期、政策冲突解决和领域本体推理。

Method: 提出AgenticRei系统，采用基于Rei框架的deontic政策语言，以OWL形式表达，并由独立于LLM的高性能逻辑引擎在运行时评估，统一管理代理工具调用和代理间通信。

Result: 实验示例显示，deontic政策能有效表达现有生产引擎无法实现的安全和隐私治理约束，且方法与A2AS等标准框架兼容。

Conclusion: AgenticRei为LLM代理提供了全面的企业治理解决方案，涵盖许可/禁止、义务、豁免和冲突解决，适用于医疗、网络安全等高风险领域。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [237] [Implicit Semantic-Aware Communication Based on Hypergraph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20162)
*Yiwei Liao,Shurui Tu,Yong Xiao,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HISR框架，利用超图表示高阶语义关系，显著提升语义通信准确性和鲁棒性，在噪声条件下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解决方案采用图结构仅捕捉成对关系，忽略了高阶隐式相关性（如群体交互、多实体关联），导致语义表达性降低和性能下降，尤其在噪声或损坏信道条件下。

Method: 本文提出HISR（基于超图的隐式语义推理框架），使用超图表示复杂多实体关系；将实体及其相关高阶关系映射到针对特定关系上下文定制的语义子空间，以解耦多样化语义交互并缓解传统图嵌入中的过度平滑效应。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的HISR在隐式语义解释准确性上比最先进基准提高高达36.6%。

Conclusion: HISR通过超图有效表示高阶语义关系，实现了鲁棒语义推断，即使在传输过程中部分信息丢失时也能维持高性能。

Abstract: Semantic-aware communication has emerged as a transformative paradigm for next-generation communication systems, shifting the fundamental goal from transmitting bit-level symbols to reliably recovering and understanding the semantic meaning of information. Previous studies have demonstrated that representing the semantic content of source messages as graph-based structures can significantly improve communication efficiency and the accuracy of semantic inference at the receiver. However, existing solutions typically employ graphs that capture only pairwise relationships, thereby neglecting higher-order implicit correlations commonly observed in real-world scenarios, such as group interactions, multi-entity associations, and complex relational contexts. This limitation reduces semantic expressiveness and makes semantic inference susceptible to ambiguity and performance degradation, particularly under noisy or corrupted channel conditions. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel hypergraph-based implicit semantic reasoning framework, HISR, which leverages hypergraphs to represent complex multi-entity relationships among semantic knowledge entities. In HISR, entities and their associated higher-order relations are mapped into dedicated semantic subspaces tailored to distinct relational contexts. This design not only disentangles diverse semantic interactions to mitigate the over-smoothing effects commonly found in traditional graph embedding methods but also enables robust semantic inference even when partial information loss occurs during transmission. Numerical results show that the proposed HISR achieves up to a 36.6% improvement in implicit semantic interpretation accuracy over the state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [238] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
*Sherzod Turaev,Mary John,Saja Aldabet,Mamoun Awad,Nazar Zaki,Khaled Shuaib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Developed a human-in-the-loop pipeline to measure CS program coverage of international curricula guidelines (CS2013 & CS2023). Found stable ~50% coverage over decade; new guidelines raise depth expectations, causing performance gap.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Undergraduate computer science programs lack reliable, reproducible methods to assess coverage completeness of current international curricular guidelines and track shifts when guidelines are restructured.

Method: Proposed a human-in-the-loop pipeline: represent program and guidelines as structured corpora, generate candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches via semantic retrieval, and confirm through human judgment under explicit coverage definition. Evaluated seven retrievers; reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble performed best.

Result: Program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, remaining near-constant over a decade. For competency articulation, ~88% of covered units meet requirements under both guidelines. Cognitive depth delivery: 76% at recommended depth for CS2023 versus 95% for CS2013, reflecting higher expectations in newer guidelines rather than program deficiency.

Conclusion: The reusable instrument identifies persistent structural gaps (e.g., parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages) independent of guideline changes, while distinguishing evolution-driven differences. Validated with inter-rater reliability (Cohen's kappa 0.64–0.69).

Abstract: Undergraduate computer science is governed by international curricular guidelines revised about once a decade, yet programs lack a reliable, reproducible way to measure how completely they cover the current guidelines and how that coverage shifts when the guidelines are restructured. We address this with a human-in-the-loop pipeline that measures a program's coverage of an external body of knowledge, applied longitudinally to one accredited BSc in Computer Science against Computer Science Curricula 2013 (CS2013) and 2023 (CS2023). The pipeline represents the program and each guideline as structured corpora, generates candidate course-to-knowledge-unit matches by semantic retrieval, and confirms them through human judgment under an explicit coverage definition. Of seven benchmarked retrievers, a reciprocal-rank-fusion ensemble was strongest, and a reputed long-context model underperformed a small sentence model, so retriever choice must be measured. Both maps were validated by an independent second rater (Cohen's kappa 0.64 for CS2023, 0.69 for CS2013). The program covers 49.7% of CS2023 and 50.9% of CS2013 knowledge units, near-constant across a decade. Extending the same retrieve-then-confirm design to competency articulation and cognitive depth shows that the program articulates the competency for ~88% of covered units under each guideline, yet delivers it at the recommended depth for 76% of present units under CS2023 against 95% under CS2013, a gap reflecting the newer guideline's raised expectations, not the program. The longitudinal comparison separates persistent structural gaps (parallel and distributed computing, foundations of programming languages, systems fundamentals), uncovered against both guidelines and ABET, from differences that reflect the standard's evolution. The instrument is reusable and available from the authors on request.

</details>


### [239] [Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475)
*Thomas Bertolani,Davide Bucciarelli,Leonardo Zini,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统评估了8个扩散语言模型（DLMs）在推理、编码等8项任务上的性能与计算效率，揭示关键推理因素对结果的影响及性能与资源消耗的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前DLMs因评估协议、数据集、推理预算和超参数设置差异导致横向比较困难，需统一框架明确其能力边界与技术取舍。

Method: 1. 跨8个基准测试评估8个SOTA DLMs；2. 分析去噪步数/上下文长度/块大小等推理参数影响；3. 控制变量对比小型模型训练效果；4. 综合衡量生成质量与计算开销。

Result: DLM性能高度依赖生成时设计选择，在特定任务中存在性能提升与计算成本增加的非线性关系，不同架构对资源约束敏感度差异显著。

Conclusion: 该研究为DLM的实际部署提供量化依据，指出需根据具体应用场景平衡推理精度与计算可行性，并强调标准化评估体系的重要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.

</details>


### [240] [Hidden Anchors in Multi-Agent LLM Deliberation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19494)
*Apurba Pokharel,Ram Dantu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A closed-loop dynamical system model reveals hidden internal beliefs (anchors) in multi-agent LLM deliberation that can be recovered and explain confidence exceeding initial convex hull.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Classical opinion-dynamics models fail to account for individual internal beliefs, limiting understanding of multi-agent deliberation where agents surpass initial consensus bounds.

Method: Proposed a closed-loop dynamical system with hidden anchors; recovered anchors from deliberation data; tested generalization on held-out runs.

Result: Anchors are recoverable; they enable confidence to escape the convex hull; influence strength is similar but anchor position varies across model families, affecting whether full model is needed.

Conclusion: Multi-agent deliberation behavior is spectrum-dependent; only when anchors are distant from initial opinions does deliberation require the complete closed-loop model.

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM deliberation, where agents exchange and revise answers over several rounds, is increasingly used to improve reasoning and accuracy, yet how and why it works is rarely modelled. Such deliberation mirrors how humans reach decisions. As social animals we are pulled both by the group, the herd effect that classical opinion-dynamics models such as DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen capture, and by our own internal belief, which they do not. We model multi-agent deliberation as a closed-loop dynamical system in which each agent carries a hidden internal belief, its anchor, that continually pulls its opinion regardless of its neighbours. We show this anchor can be recovered from the deliberation alone, and that it explains a behaviour classical consensus rules forbid: an agent's confidence in the correct answer can climb past where any agent started, escaping the space (convexhull) formed by the initial beliefs. Checking whether the recovered anchor also predicts held-out runs (generalizes) gives a simple test for when a model is truly driven bysuch an anchor. Across three open-weight model families this is a spectrum, not all-or-nothing. All anchors' influence are about equally strongly, but they differ in where the anchor sits, and only when it sits far from the initial opinions does deliberation escape the hull and need the full closed-loop model.

</details>


### [241] [DeXposure-Claw: An Agentic System for DeFi Risk Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19501)
*Aijie Shu,Bowei Chen,Wenbin Wu,Cathy Yi-Hsuan Chen,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeXposure-Claw系统，解决去中心化金融监管中LLM代理过度解读弱证据和误报问题，通过图时间序列基础模型、确定性监控和置信度门控机制，实现可审计的监管监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化金融暴露监管者于快速变化的网络化信用风险，而通用LLM代理在此场景下表现不佳：过度解读弱证据并推荐高风险干预，且现有评估缺乏与监管者对齐的误报测量方式。

Method: 系统包含三个核心组件：(1) DeXposure-FM图时间序列基础模型预测未来暴露网络；(2) 确定性监控器和压力情景将预测转化为类型化警报、归因信号和情景证据；(3) 数据健康和置信度门控在发出可审计监督票据前限制升级。配套开发DeXposure-Bench六轴评估框架，决策轴基于监管者对齐的绝对损失基线和错误干预率评分。

Result: 在五年每周真实数据上的实验完全支持该系统，证明DeXposure-Claw能够有效减少误报并提供可审计的监管监督。

Conclusion: DeXposure-Claw通过结构化证据路由LLM决策，解决了去中心化金融监管中的信用风险监测问题，为监管者提供了更可靠、可审计的自动化监督工具。

Abstract: Decentralized finance exposes supervisors to fast-moving, networked credit risks. General-purpose LLM agents fit this setting poorly: they over-read weak evidence and recommend high-stakes interventions, while existing evaluations offer no regulator-aligned way to measure the resulting false alarms. We introduce DeXposure-Claw, a forecast-grounded agentic supervision system that routes LLM decisions through structured evidence: (1) DeXposure-FM, a graph time-series foundation model, forecasts future exposure networks; (2) deterministic monitors and stress scenarios then turn those forecasts into typed alerts, attribution signals, and scenario evidence; and (3) data-health and confidence gates constrain escalation before DeXposure-Claw emits auditable supervisory tickets with rationales. We further develop DeXposure-Bench, a six-axis evaluation harness, whose decision axis scores tickets against a regulator-aligned absolute-loss ground truth and an explicit false-intervention rate. Experiments on five years of weekly real data fully support our system. Code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/DeXposure-Claw.

</details>


### [242] [LLM Doesn't Know What It Doesn't Know: Detecting Epistemic Blind Spots via Cross-Model Attribution Divergence on Clinical Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19509)
*Akshat Dasula,Prasanna Desikan,Jaideep Srivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示大语言模型（LLM）在结构化临床任务中存在认知盲区：其口头置信度无法反映真实准确性，且与XGBoost呈现反向难度效应。通过少样本示例与SHAP特征证据的协同干预及跨模型校准器，可显著提升可靠性估计精度，为解决LLM在结构化数据上的"冷启动"问题提供路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在处理结构化临床数据时能否识别自身知识边界，以解决其在高风险医疗场景中因认知不确定性导致的决策风险。

Method: 采用跨模型归因分歧分析框架，对比Qwen 2.5 7B与XGBoost在结构化预测任务中的表现，结合少样本示例、SHAP特征解释与跨模型校准器进行干预实验。

Result: 1. LLM口头置信度呈认知无效性（0.856-0.937恒定输出）；2. 存在反向难度效应（XGBoost高置信时LLM准确率降至64.8%）；3. 少样本+SHAP干预使ADS降低75.3%、准确率提升53.7%；4. 跨模型校准器将校准误差降低68.5%并生成患者特异性可靠性估计。

Conclusion: 提出LLM在结构化数据应用中的"冷启动"理论框架，证明通过外部归因信号可实现无需模型微调的认知自感知，为医疗AI安全部署建立新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to structured clinical data, yet whether they can recognize the limits of their own knowledge on such tasks remains unexplored. We study this question through the lens of cross-model attribution divergence with the goal of reducing epistemic uncertainty for structured tasks, comparing Qwen 2.5 7B and XGBoost on a prediction task via attribution divergence analysis. We report four findings. First, LLM verbalized confidence is epistemically vacuous, it outputs a near-constant (0.856-0.937) regardless of whether accuracy is 49% or 75.3%, tracking prompt format rather than prediction quality. Second, the LLM exhibits an inverse difficulty effect: accuracy drops to 64.8% when XGBoost is 99% correct, but matches XGBoost (73.8% vs. 73.1%) when it is moderately uncertain. Third, few-shot examples and SHAP-derived feature evidence are orthogonal, super-additive interventions: they reduce the Attribution Disagreement Score (ADS) from 1.54 to 0.38 and improve accuracy from 49% to 75.3% without training. Fourth, a cross-model calibrator that determined LLM reliability using attribution divergence signals reduces expected calibration error from 0.254 to 0.080, replacing uninformative verbalized confidence with patient-specific reliability estimates, without accessing model internals or requiring repeated inference. We frame these findings as a cold start problem for LLMs on structured data and outline a path toward genuine epistemic self-awareness.

</details>


### [243] [REVEAL++: Differentiable Phenotypic Grouping for Vision-Language Retinal Modeling of Alzheimer's Disease Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19522)
*Ethan Elio Meidinger,Seowung Leem,Zeyun Zhao,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出连续表型结构对比学习框架，通过视网膜图像和临床风险叙事改进阿尔茨海默病早期预测，在UK Biobank数据上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用离散表型分组，导致刚性监督和与表示学习解耦，无法捕捉疾病风险的连续谱系特性。

Method: 将表型相似性建模为可微权重函数，基于视网膜图像和风险剖面的模态内嵌入相似性，定义软多正关系，并引入软目标对比目标进行端到端学习。

Result: 在UK Biobank视网膜成像数据上的实验表明，该方法一致地优于离散分组对比学习和标准视觉-语言基线。

Conclusion: 通过将表型相似性视为可学习连续信号而非固定分组规则，为多模态视网膜和临床数据的神经退行性风险建模提供了原则性和鲁棒的基础。

Abstract: The retina offers a noninvasive window into neurodegenerative disease, capturing subtle structural patterns associated with a risk of future cognitive decline. Vision-language alignment frameworks such as REVEAL have shown that pairing retinal fundus images with structured clinical risk narratives improves early prediction of Alzheimer's disease (AD). A key design choice in these approaches is the use of phenotypic grouping, where individuals with similar risk profiles are treated as multi-positive pairs during contrastive learning. However, existing methods operationalize phenotypic similarity as a discrete construct, relying on hard group assignments that impose rigid supervision and decouple group formation from representation learning. We propose a continuous formulation of phenotypic structure within contrastive learning. Rather than assigning samples to fixed clusters, we model inter-subject similarity as a differentiable weighting function derived from intra-modality embedding similarities in both retinal images and risk profiles. These weights define soft multi-positive relationships through a continuous aggregation operator, enabling graded supervision that reflects the spectrum nature of disease risk. We further introduce a soft-target contrastive objective that jointly learns cross-modal alignment and phenotypic structure in an end-to-end manner. Evaluated on UK Biobank retinal imaging data for incident AD prediction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms discrete group-based contrastive learning and standard vision-language baselines. By treating phenotypic similarity as a learnable, continuous signal rather than a fixed grouping rule, our approach provides a principled and robust foundation for population-scale neurodegenerative risk modeling from multi-modal retinal and clinical data.

</details>


### [244] [Emergent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19527)
*Martin Kolář*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Can Large Language Models (LLMs) discern when their own outputs are misaligned with human ethics? And can they self-correct? We endow an LLM with a conscience step that reviews its own reasoning and outputs, and we extend the training loss with an alignment component using Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer the model away from non-ethical outputs. The result is an online technique to align models in a wide range of applications: training, fine-tuning, adversarial prompting, and zero-shot learning. It does not require a weaker or stronger judge, relying instead on a frozen copy of itself. In previous work, the Emergent Misalignment scenario showed a range of emergent unethical behaviors from fine-tuning the model to hack code. Instead, we empirically show how to achieve Emergent Alignment: a single high-level introspective question steers training toward an ethical model under the same code hacking scenario.

</details>


### [245] [ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538)
*Ashim Dhor,Rasel Mondal,Pin Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ITNet提出统一架构，将卷积、循环和Transformer视为可学习积分变换的特例，实现高效通用近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络架构（卷积、循环、Transformer）数学上分离，但作者认为这源于对单一数学对象（可学习积分变换）的不完整理解，旨在解决碎片化问题。

Method: 设计ITNet，核心为可学习核（MLP实现），建模位置和特征依赖的成对交互；引入tiled kernel fusion、重要性加权蒙特卡洛积分和 learned low-rank factorization 提升效率。

Result: ITNet在参数化下重现卷积、自注意力、循环网络；作为连续算子通用近似器；在ImageNet-1K、GLUE等多个基准测试中匹配或超越专用模型。

Conclusion: 实验证明单一学习交互机制能从数据中恢复所有三大架构家族行为，验证了统一框架的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Convolutional networks, recurrent networks, and transformers each encode different inductive biases -- locality, sequential memory, and content-dependent pairwise interaction -- and have remained mathematically distinct since their inception. We show that this fragmentation reflects not a fundamental diversity in how signals should be processed, but rather incomplete views of a single underlying mathematical object: a learnable integral transform. We introduce the Integral Transform Network (ITNet), a unified architecture built around a learnable kernel that depends jointly on positions and features. This kernel is implemented as a small neural network, specifically an MLP, that models pairwise interactions, enabling the model to adapt its behavior from data. We show that convolution, self-attention (including multi-head), and autoregressive recurrence (including LSTM, GRU, S4, and Mamba) arise as special cases under appropriate parameterizations, and that ITNet is a universal approximator of continuous operators. To make this practical, we develop tiled kernel fusion, importance-weighted Monte Carlo integration, and learned low-rank factorization, enabling efficient and scalable computation. A single ITNet architecture with a shared operator and lightweight modality-specific encoders matches or exceeds specialized baselines on ImageNet-1K , GLUE, ModelNet40, VQA\,v2 and NLVR2. The results demonstrate that a single learned interaction mechanism can recover the behavior of all three architectural families from data.

</details>


### [246] [Uncertainty Decomposition for Clarification Seeking in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19559)
*Gregory Matsnev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种提示式不确定性分解方法，分离动作置信度与请求不确定性，在澄清增强的LLM代理基准测试中显著提升性能，平均在ALFWorld-Clarification上F1分数较基线提高36%-73%，且泛化至多个主流LLM骨干。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典aleatoric/epistemic不确定性框架无法满足交互式LLM代理需求（如主动澄清和共享心智模型构建）；实际部署约束（黑盒API、交互延迟限制、缺乏标记轨迹）排除了logprob-based、multi-sampling和training-based方法，亟需开发prompt-based的不确定性表示方案。

Method: 设计简单提示式分解机制，将动作置信度（action confidence）与请求不确定性（u）解耦，使代理能识别任务规范模糊性并触发澄清请求；在WebShop-Clarification和ALFWorld-Clarification基准（50%任务故意underspecified）上系统评估，对比ReAct+UE和Uncertainty-Aware Memory (UAM)，覆盖GPT-5.1、DeepSeek-v3.2-exp、GLM-4.7、Qwen3.5-35B、GPT-OSS-120B五个LLM骨干。

Result: 跨五个骨干平均：ALFWorld-Clarification上澄清F1较ReAct+UE提升73%、较UAM提升36%；WebShop-Clarification上所有骨干均取得最优F1，ALFWorld-Clarification上四个骨干领先，证实方法泛化性超越单一模型依赖。

Conclusion: 该提示式分解有效解决了交互场景中的不确定性表征瓶颈，显著增强LLM代理的澄清能力，为部署约束下的实用化交互式代理提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Recent position papers argue that the classical aleatoric/epistemic uncertainty framework is insufficient for interactive large language model (LLM) agents and call for underspecification-aware, decomposed, and communicable uncertainty representations that can unlock new agent capabilities such as proactive clarification seeking and shared mental-model building. Practical deployment constraints -- black-box APIs, interactive latency budgets, and the absence of labeled trajectories -- rule out logprob-based, multi-sampling, and training-based methods, leaving prompt-based estimation as the most viable family for surfacing such signals at deployment time. We answer this call with a simple prompt-based decomposition that separates action confidence from request uncertainty (u), enabling the agent to ask for clarification when the task specification is ambiguous. To evaluate it, we introduce two clarification-augmented benchmarks (WebShop-Clarification and ALFWorld-Clarification) in which 50% of tasks are deliberately underspecified, and systematically compare the proposed decomposition against ReAct+UE and Uncertainty-Aware Memory (UAM) across five LLM backbones (GPT-5.1, DeepSeek-v3.2-exp, GLM-4.7, Qwen3.5-35B, GPT-OSS-120B) on these variants together with the standard WebShop, ALFWorld, and REAL benchmarks for fault detection. Averaged across the five backbones, the proposed decomposition improves clarification F1 on ALFWorld-Clarification by 73% over ReAct+UE and by 36% over UAM, and leads clarification F1 on every backbone on WebShop-Clarification and on four of five backbones on ALFWorld-Clarification, indicating that the gains generalize beyond a single LLM.

</details>


### [247] [Analyzing the Narration Gap in LLM-Solver Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19588)
*Zunchen Huang,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM-求解器混合推理管道中"叙述"步骤的安全性问题，发现尽管形式化工具本身可靠，但攻击者可通过提示注入在最终用户答案中反转已验证结论，现有缓解方法无法完全消除该风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化工具（SAT/SMT求解器）被嵌入LLM推理管道以处理安全关键问题，求解器提供独立可验证的可靠答案，但在LLM与求解器的交互过程中，可靠性保证可能丢失。现有研究关注形式化和决策步骤，但忽略了将求解器输出转化为用户答案的"叙述"步骤，该步骤存在安全漏洞。

Method: 1. 将LLM-求解器循环建模为已验证的决策过程；2. 在提示注入攻击下评估五个开源模型；3. 测试证书门控机制和强化提示等缓解方案；4. 结合形式化分析与实证研究评估最终用户答案的可靠性。

Result: 证书门控使求解器裁决具有可靠性，但攻击者可在不同表述和渠道中反转已验证结论；强化提示显著减少注入攻击，但无法完全消除，且在自适应攻击下仍存在漏洞；最终用户答案的可靠性未得到保障。

Conclusion: 在LLM-求解器混合推理管道中，形式化工具的可靠性保证无法传递到用户最终阅读的答案层面，提示注入攻击可通过叙述步骤破坏已验证结论，现有缓解方法不足以提供端到端的安全保障。

Abstract: Formal tools such as SAT and SMT solvers are increasingly embedded in language model reasoning pipelines when a safety or security critical question can be formulated in logic. Unlike chain of thought whose steps are sampled from the model distribution without formal guarantee, a solver produces a sound and independently verifiable answer. However, the soundness guarantee can be lost in the interaction between the solver and the model. The hybrid pipeline has three components: formalizing the question, deciding it, and narrating the result. Prior work has studied the formalization and decision, but not narration, which is the step that turns a formal tool's output into the user answer. To fill the narration gap, we first model the LLM-solver loop as a verified decision procedure. We further evaluate five open-sourced models under prompt injection, and we find certificate gating makes the solver verdict sound, while an adversary can invert a verified conclusion across phrasings and channels. We study the mitigation through hardened prompt that reduces injection significantly but cannot eliminate it and still suffers under adaptive attack. Combining the formal analysis and empirical studies, we show in the LLM-solver loop, robustness does not reach to the answer that the user finally reads.

</details>


### [248] [Configurable Clinical Information Extraction with Agentic RAG: What Works, What Breaks, and Why](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19602)
*Osman Alperen Çinar-Koraş,Marie Bauer,Sameh Khattab,Merlin Engelke,Moon Kim,Stephan Settelmeier,Shigeyasu Sugawara,Fabian Freisleben,Felix Nensa,Jens Kleesiek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出ACIE系统，解决临床数据中元数据缺失问题，通过本地化代理RAG流程实现高准确率的信息提取，并经临床医生验证达到96.5%接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者数据分散于异构文档且元数据缺失，传统RAG方法难以处理时间推理与跨文档依赖，影响临床决策支持系统的可靠性。

Method: 部署本地化ACIE系统，采用代理RAG架构整合完整患者上下文，结合独立淋巴瘤登记研究进行回溯验证，由核医学医师逐项核对提取结果与原始文献。

Result: 在7,326个判断中，临床医生接受96.5%的提取结果，各类型提取接受率为80%-99%，显著优于传统RAG方法。

Conclusion: ACIE系统有效弥合临床元数据缺口，其代理RAG架构具备强鲁棒性，为医疗AI提供可验证的知识基础，推动精准临床决策支持发展。

Abstract: Patient contexts span hundreds of heterogeneous documents and thousands of structured data points, yet the document-level metadata that AI systems need for retrieval and triage is absent or incomplete. Standard retrieval-augmented generation fails on this data, mishandling temporal reasoning, cross-document dependencies, and missing metadata. We deploy ACIE (Agentic Clinical Information Extraction) at University Medicine Essen: an on-premise agentic RAG pipeline that reasons over complete patient contexts and grounds every answer in source passages for clinician verification. We quantify the metadata gap, trace the architectural decisions it shaped, and evaluate extraction alongside an independent retrospective lymphoma registry study, in which nuclear-medicine physicians verify every extracted value against its cited sources. Across 7,326 judgments, clinicians accepted 96.5\% of extractions, with per-type acceptance ranging from 80\% to 99\%.

</details>


### [249] [Which Pairs to Compare for LLM Post-Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19607)
*Jiangze Han,Vineet Goyal,Will Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究在基于偏好的后训练中如何选择最有信息量的比较对，以优化DPO训练。通过公式化采样设计问题，提供理论界限并实验验证，证明提出的设计提高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好标签成本高昂，而生成额外完成项成本较低。因此，论文提出重新分配标签预算：生成更大池的完成项，但仅标记最信息量的比较对，以提升样本效率。

Method: 将比较策划公式化为采样设计问题，评估设计通过最终策略质量。针对Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)实例化框架，分析标记对选择如何影响下游策略性能。提供DPO训练策略的后训练最优性间隙的匹配上界和下界，揭示设计依赖的信息矩阵机制。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 匹配的上界和下界 on the post-training optimality gap of DPO-trained policy；(2) 比较选择通过设计依赖的信息矩阵影响下游性能，链接标签分配到参数估计误差和策略次优性；(3) 产生明确的优化标准用于预算化比较策划；(4) 实验在合成设置和语言模型基准测试上证明，提出的设计一致优于常见启发式，提高样本效率。

Conclusion: 比较选择是影响后训练性能的关键因素，可通过信息矩阵优化。实用采样设计能有效提升样本效率，为预算化比较策划提供理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: Preference-based post-training has become a central paradigm for aligning language models. A common data-collection strategy is to generate a small set of completions for each prompt and label the resulting comparison pairs. However, human preference labels are often much more expensive than generating additional completions, suggesting a different use of the same labeling budget: generate a larger pool of completions, but label only the most informative comparison pairs. This paper studies which pairs should be compared in preference-based post-training. We formulate comparison curation as a sampling-design problem and evaluate designs by the quality of the final policy under the preference-based post-training objective. We instantiate this framework for Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), analyzing how the choice of labeled pairs propagates through DPO training to downstream policy performance. Our main results provide matching upper and lower bounds on the post-training optimality gap of the DPO-trained policy. The bounds show that comparison selection affects downstream performance through a single design-dependent information matrix, which links label allocation to parameter estimation error and policy suboptimality. This yields an explicit optimization criterion for budgeted comparison curation and motivates practical sampling designs for selecting informative pairs from large generated completion pools. Experiments on synthetic settings and language-model post-training benchmarks show that the proposed designs consistently improve sample efficiency over common comparison-selection heuristics.

</details>


### [250] [Toten: Knowledge-Based Ontological Tokenization Of Physical Quantities And Technical Notation In Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19626)
*Antonio de Sousa Leitão Filho; Allan Kardec Duailibe Barros Filho; Fabrício Saul Lima; Selby Mykael Lima dos Santos; Rejani Bandeira Vieira Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Byte-Pair Encoding tokenization is statistically efficient for vocabulary compression, but semantically blind to structured technical entities, fragmenting physical quantities, numbers, units, and symbolic expressions into lexically arbitrary subwords. We present TOTEN, a knowledge-based ontological tokenization framework that replaces statistical derivation with declarative classification grounded in a formal ontology of engineering entities (OEE). We formalize TOTEN as the triple <O, classify, {inst_tau}>: the ontology gathers types, structural principles, composition relations, and preservable invariants; the classification function maps raw text into typed regions; and the instantiator family yields a self-descriptive structured representation. Robustness derives from deterministic coupling with three external oracles: Pint (dimensional), Unicode Character Database (typographic), and RSLP (Portuguese morphology). Intrinsic evaluation covers four properties verifiable by construction -- ontological atomicity, dimensional equivalence, typographic robustness, and numerical reconstruction -- over an internal, physically validated benchmark (EngQuant, N=800) and four Brazilian Portuguese external corpora (N=1771 eligible cases). We also report detection recall, distinguishing coverage from conditional atomicity. Against eight state-of-the-art baselines, TOTEN achieves unit ontological atomicity in all contrasts and numerical reconstruction of 0.775-0.904 on external corpora, vs. 0.627-0.703 for the best baseline (Quantulum3); on EngQuant, 0.780 vs. 0.340. Differences are statistically significant (McNemar with Holm correction). Spearman correlation between internal and external rankings confirms concurrent validity of the control benchmark. Dimensional equivalence shows statistical parity with Pint, the oracle from which the system inherits dimensional authority.

</details>


### [251] [AI4SE and SE4AI Exploration: A Decade Looking Back and Forward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19630)
*H. Sinan Bank,Daniel R. Herber,Thomas Bradley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The March 2020 INCOSE INSIGHT special issue on AI and Systems Engineering (SE) became the most downloaded issue in the publication's history and launched a research community that now draws over 250 registrants to its annual workshop. In this article, we trace the progress in AI and SE across three phases (labeled here foundational, applied, and LLM inflection) based on the authors' reading of the field's core papers, and describe our opinions of where the community has converged and where critical gaps remain. Separately, a human-AI agreement literature review leveraging both human expertise and six AI models was performed to assess the relevance of 1,712 INCOSE INSIGHT articles and 889 SERC publications. The results identify five critical research gaps and offer guidance for practitioners navigating AI adoption, assurance, and workforce transformation in SE. We share the agreement data and the AI4SE/SE4AI Explorer web application so readers can compare their own relevance judgments with the human and AI raters.

</details>


### [252] [BrainG3N: A Dual-Purpose Tokenizer for Controllable 3D Brain MRI Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19651)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于3D脑MRI潜在扩散的全体积掩码自编码器(MAE)分词器，通过解耦编码器和解码器，在35,309个体积的预训练后，在23项任务基准测试中21项优于或匹配现有SOTA模型，同时支持条件生成和患者特异性纵向预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D脑MRI在临床神经病学和神经肿瘤学中至关重要，生成模型可用于增强代表性不足的队列、模拟疾病轨迹和支持隐私保护的数据共享。然而现有重建驱动的分词器在保留临床信息和重建解剖忠实体积之间存在竞争需求

Method: 引入全体积掩码自编码器(MAE)分词器，解耦编码器和解码器：冻结的3D MAE编码器产生临床信息嵌入，专用CNN解码器从这些嵌入的线性投影重建体素。在18个公开队列、4种模态、10种疾病类别和200+采集位点的35,309个体积上预训练编码器

Result: 在23项线性探测基准测试中，编码器在21项任务上优于或匹配SOTA模型(BrainIAC、BrainSegFounder和MedicalNet)。基于这些临床信息嵌入的条件扩散Transformer(DiT)支持6个变量的条件生成和患者特异性纵向预测

Conclusion: 建立了单一的3D脑MRI嵌入空间，能够同时支持下游临床任务和控制生成，解决了现有方法中临床信息保留与解剖重建之间的权衡问题

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) brain MRI is central to clinical neurology and neuro-oncology, where generative models could augment under-represented cohorts, simulate disease trajectories, and support privacy-preserving data sharing. Latent diffusion has been the go-to solution for modeling imaging data, but it places two competing demands on the tokenizer: encoder embeddings must retain the clinical information that downstream tasks act on, and the decoder must reconstruct anatomically faithful volumes. Existing reconstruction-driven tokenizers achieve the second at the expense of the first. To address this, we introduce a fully volumetric masked-autoencoder (MAE) based tokenizer for 3D brain MRI latent diffusion, decoupling encoder and decoder: a frozen 3D MAE encoder produces clinically informative embeddings, while a dedicated CNN decoder reconstructs voxels from a linear projection of those embeddings. We pretrain the encoder on 35,309 volumes from 18 public cohorts spanning four modalities, ten disease categories, and 200+ acquisition sites, and demonstrate its dual utility in two settings. First, on a 23-task linear-probing benchmark, the encoder outperforms or matches SOTA models (i.e., BrainIAC, BrainSegFounder, and MedicalNet) on 21 of 23 tasks. Second, a conditional diffusion transformer (DiT) trained on these clinically informative embeddings supports both conditional generation across six variables and patient-specific longitudinal forecasting. Together these results establish a single 3D brain-MRI embedding space capable of both downstream clinical tasks and controllable generation.

</details>


### [253] [Denoising Implicit Feedback for Cold-start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19658)
*Gaode Chen,Shicheng Wang,Shikun Li,Rui Huang,Xinghua Zhang,Yunze Luo,Shipeng Li,Shiming Ge,Ruina Sun,Yinjie Jiang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DIF方法通过内容相似性推断伪标签，自适应去噪冷启动物品，显著提升推荐系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式反馈在推荐系统中普遍存在噪声（如点击诱饵、位置偏差），冷物品更易受噪声影响，现有去噪方法基于启发式模式（如高损失值），在冷启动场景适应性差且效果有限。

Method: DIF是一种模型无关的去噪方法：1) 利用用户内容偏好稳定性，通过内容相似热物品推断伪标签（指示用户兴趣）；2) 建模伪标签置信度基于冷-热物品内容相似度，聚合多伪标签；3) 显式估计噪声样本标签不确定性（考虑相对熵和冷启动状态），自适应指导伪标签在样本级别纠正噪声。

Result: 理论证明和大量真实数据集实验验证DIF优越性；已部署于快手（十亿用户规模短视频应用），在冷启动场景下显著提升多项商业指标。

Conclusion: DIF有效解决了冷启动物品的隐式反馈去噪问题，具有强适应性和实际部署价值，为推荐系统冷启动优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Implicit feedback is widely used in recommender systems due to its accessibility and generality, yet it usually presents noisy samples (e.g., clickbait, position bias). Meanwhile, recommenders inevitably face the item cold-start problem due to the continuous influx of new items. We identify that cold items are more prone to noisy samples due to the aforementioned factors, and researchers often overlook the significance of denoising implicit feedback for cold items. Previous denoising studies usually identify noisy samples based on heuristic patterns, such as higher loss values, and mitigate noise through sample selection or re-weighting. However, these methods have limited adaptability and are ineffective in cold-start scenarios. To achieve denoising implicit feedback for cold-start recommendation, we propose a model-agnostic denoising method called DIF. First, user preferences for content remain stable, which allows us to infer pseudo-labels indicating whether a user is interested in a cold item through content-similar warm items. Furthermore, to improve pseudo-label accuracy, we model the confidence of pseudo-labels based on the content similarity between the cold item and warm items, and then aggregate multiple pseudo-labels for each sample. Finally, we explicitly estimate the uncertainty of the noisy sample label by considering its relative entropy and the cold-start status of the item, which adaptively guides the role of pseudo-labels to correct the noisy labels at the sample level. DIF's superiority is supported by both theoretical justification and extensive experiments on real-world datasets. The method has been deployed on a billion-user scale short video application Kuaishou and has significantly improved various commercial metrics within cold-start scenarios.

</details>


### [254] [Exit-and-Join Dynamics for Decentralized Coalition Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19683)
*Quanyan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究联盟形成的分散动态过程，由单边退出和加入决策驱动，使用Aumann-Dreze值评估局部移动，连接合作收益分配与非合作最佳响应行为，分析稳定性及成本影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于理解联盟形成如何作为动态过程运作，其中代理基于局部信息做决策，从而桥接合作博弈中的收益分配与非合作博弈中的行为，填补分散决策下稳定性分析的空白。

Method: 方法包括构建理论模型，代理使用Aumann-Dreze值计算联盟内收益；推导均衡特征化（终端分区对应无个体有利可图偏差的状态）；识别动态 admit 标量Lyapunov函数或精确势表示的数学条件；分析切换和接受成本对局部稳定性的影响；通过数值实验验证有限时间稳定化、成本敏感性及凸博弈基准。

Result: 结果显示终端分区严格等价于无个体有利可图退出-加入偏差的联盟结构；成功建立均衡特征化；确定了动态收敛的充分条件（如Lyapunov稳定性）；数值实验证实有限时间稳定化可行性、成本参数对稳定性的影响，并在凸博弈中验证理论。

Conclusion: 结论是该模型有效整合了合作与非合作博弈框架，为联盟动态提供了理论基础，证明局部决策机制可导向全局稳定，并量化了成本在稳定性塑造中的关键作用。

Abstract: This paper studies coalition formation as a decentralized dynamical process driven by unilateral exit-and-join decisions. Agents evaluate local moves using the Aumann-Dreze value, so payoffs are computed within the agent's current coalition rather than through a globally negotiated coalition structure. The resulting model links cooperative payoff allocation with noncooperative best-response behavior: a terminal partition is precisely a coalition structure with no admissible, individually profitable exit-and-join deviation. We establish equilibrium characterizations, identify conditions under which the dynamics admit scalar Lyapunov or exact-potential representations, and analyze how switching and acceptance costs shape local stability. Numerical experiments test finite-time stabilization, cost sensitivity, and a special convex-game benchmark.

</details>


### [255] [Beyond Static Leaderboards: Predictive Validity for the Evaluation of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19704)
*Dhaval C. Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui,Shuxin Lin,Yusheng Li,Tianjun Feng,Chun-Yi Tsai,Yihan Sun,Wei Alexander Xin,Akshat Bhandari,Tanisha Rathod,Aaron Fan,Sanskruti Vijay Shejwal,Tomas Pasiecznik,Sagar Chethan Kumar,Tanmay Agarwal,Rohith Kanathur,Sam Colman,Amaan Sheikh,Dev Bahl,Ann Li,Krish Veera,Alimurtaza Mustafa Merchant,Shambhawi Baswaraj Bhure,Sajal Kumar Goyla,Chengrui Li,Kirthana Natarajan,Rui Li,Thomas Ajai,Rujing Li,Vivek G. Iyer,Sanjaii Vijayakumar,Yitong Bai,Ayal Yakobe,Darief Maes,Yassine Jebbouri,Tianyang Xu,Thai Quoc On,Vera Mazeeva,Winston Li,Yuval Shemla,Yeshitha Bhuvanesh,Rushin Bhatt,Siddharth Chethan Gowda,Alisha Vinod,Caroline Cahill,Shriya Aishani Rachakonda,Yunfeng Chen,Aryaman Agrawal,Aman Upganlawar,Mao Le Jonathan Ang,Yubin Sally Go,Madhav Rajkondawar,Yang-Jung Chen,Trisha Maturi,Ananya Kapoor,Andrew Li,Shrey Arora,Mana Abbaszadeh,Shen Li,Charles Xu,Byeolah Kwon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文批评当前智能体基准测试的聚合分数排行榜无法覆盖部署维度，提出基于预测有效性的新评估框架，通过12层测量装置和3个可证伪标准重新定义智能体评估方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准测试快速增长，但单一基准无法覆盖部署中暴露的多个维度。现有聚合分数排行榜系统性地低估了部署智能体的评估能力，排名无法转移到分布外设置，需要新的评估范式

Method: 聚合14个平行实施研究（涵盖多模态视觉扩展、编排、检索策略、推理模式等）与7个先前基准，提出基于预测有效性（样本内外排名相关性）而非样本内均值的排名配置，构建12层测量装置，通过3个可证伪分布外标准进行操作化

Result: 现有证据部分支持预测有效性优于聚合分数的立场，但证据过于薄弱无法确认。12层测量装置暴露了HELM及其智能体继任者所崩溃的部署相关维度

Conclusion: 提出预注册试点设计和领域级愿景，定义下一代智能体基准应报告的内容。强调评估应关注部署相关维度而非简单聚合分数，为智能体评估提供了更全面的框架

Abstract: Agent benchmarks are growing fast, but no single benchmark touches more than four or five of the dimensions that deployment exposes. This paper aggregates the largest coordinated deep-dive of one MCP-based industrial-agent benchmark to date: fourteen parallel implementation studies covering new asset classes (including a multi-modal visual extension), alternative orchestrations, retrieval strategies, reasoning modes, infrastructure optimizations, and evaluation-methodology probes. Consolidating those studies with seven prior agent benchmarks, we argue that aggregate-score leaderboards systematically underspecify deployed-agent evaluation. Rankings derived from aggregate scores do not transfer to out-of-distribution settings; recent public-to-hidden competition retrospectives provide direct empirical evidence of this rank instability. We propose ranking configurations by predictive validity, the correlation between in-sample and out-of-sample rank, rather than in-sample mean, and report a twelve-tier measurement apparatus that exposes the deployment-relevant dimensions HELM and its agent-era successors collapse. The position is operationalized through three falsifiable out-of-distribution criteria with explicit thresholds; existing evidence partly supports it but is too thin to confirm. We close with a pre-registered pilot design and a field-level vision for what the next generation of agentic benchmarks should report.

</details>


### [256] [GLARE: A Natural Language Interface for Querying Global Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19735)
*Bhavan Vasu,Rajesh Mangannavar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: While global explanations are crucial for understanding vision models across datasets, classes, and decision contexts, their complex and monolithic nature often hinders practical exploration. Because users typically seek targeted answers to specific questions rather than static artifacts, we present an LLM-based interactive interface that provides natural language access to global explanations for black-box image classifiers. The system's core LLM acts as a mediator, translating natural language questions into structured SQL queries over local explanation data. This enables flexible aggregation without exposing users to low-level representations. For each query, the interface outputs statistics-augmented natural language responses, supporting local explanations, and intent-aligned visualizations. We evaluate the system on intent interpretation, query mapping accuracy, generalization to novel queries and datasets, and robustness to linguistic errors. Our results demonstrate that LLM-mediated querying substantially improves the accessibility and usability of global explanations for human-centered XAI.

</details>


### [257] [Interpreting Neural Combinatorial Optimization via Evolving Programmatic Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19741)
*Haocheng Duan,Yuxin Guo,Jieyi Bi,Anqi Xie,Sirui Li,Yining Ma,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EPB是首个将黑盒神经组合优化（NCO）模型蒸馏成人可读程序组合的框架，通过LLM自主演化程序库和迭代优化机制（Block I：混合文本-数值梯度下降；Block II：动态容量调整），实现高保真可解释性并维持原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经组合优化（NCO）虽性能优异，但其黑盒性质严重阻碍了实际部署和科学诊断；现有可解释性工具如概念瓶颈模型（CBMs）因NCO决策的动态性、状态依赖性及缺乏明确概念词汇定义而无法适用。

Method: 提出Evolving Programmatic Bottlenecks (EPB)框架：1) 利用LLM自主演化程序库，以每步动作分布作为瓶颈；2) 迭代双模块设计——Block I固定程序库容量，采用混合文本-数值梯度下降（数值梯度优化学生路由器，文本梯度驱动LLM程序修订）；Block II动态调整容量（通过故障定向扩展和冗余剪枝）。

Result: 实验验证EPB在多种NCO场景下有效且泛化性强：蒸馏生成的程序组合在求解质量上高度匹配原始NCO模型；揭示了NCO行为在优化阶段呈现动态演变，并可近似为经典启发式变体的组合。

Conclusion: EPB显著推进了可解释NCO研究，为序列决策模型提供了首个高效、保真的可解释工具，兼具理论价值和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) achieves strong performance, yet its black-box nature remains a key roadblock to deployment and scientific diagnosis. Standard interpretability tools, such as Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs), are ill-equipped for NCO, whose decisions are dynamic, state-dependent, and lack proper concept vocabulary definition. To close this gap, we introduce Evolving Programmatic Bottlenecks (EPB), to our knowledge, the first framework for interpreting NCO policies by distilling black-box NCO models into human-readable program portfolios. EPB employs an LLM to autonomously evolve a bank of programs, where each program's per-step action distribution serves as the bottleneck. EPB works through an iterative framework: Block I fixes program bank capacity and introduces a hybrid textual-numerical gradient descent scheme that couples numerical gradients for student router updates and textual gradients for LLM-based program revision; Block II dynamically adapts bank capacity via fault-targeted expansion and redundancy pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate EPB's effectiveness and broad applicability, where the distilled program portfolios largely match original performance. EPB also reveals that NCO behavior shifts across optimization stages and can be approximated as a composition of classic heuristic variants. Our work advances interpretable NCO and establishes EPB as a promising tool for interpreting sequential decision-making models.

</details>


### [258] [A Comparative Study of Pretrained Transformer Models for Quranic ASR: Speech Representations, Label Formats, and Dataset Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19747)
*Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Riasat Islam,Unaizah Obaidellah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过微调预训练Transformer模型（Wav2Vec2.0、HuBERT、XLS-R）实现古兰经自动语音识别（ASR）的显著改进，WER降至0.08-0.11，训练时间从140小时缩减至40小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR模型在用户诵读古兰经时Word Error Rate（WER）高且缺乏古兰经语料库完整覆盖，需提升转录准确性和效率。

Method: 系统实证研究：使用自监督学习模型（Wav2Vec2.0、HuBERT、XLS-R）在过滤后的870+小时古兰经数据集（含专业和用户诵读）上进行领域微调；通过消融实验分析特征提取器、输出标签格式、训练策略及音频片段时长的影响。

Result: 最佳配置WER为0.08（EveryAyah子集）和0.11（EveryAyah+Tarteel设置），较Citrinet基线（WER=0.163）提升约5个百分点；训练时间从140小时降至40小时；阿拉伯语无变音符号文本微调效果最佳；Wav2Vec2-XLSR-53提供最强整体表示。

Conclusion: 关键影响因素包括特征提取器选择（Wav2Vec2-XLSR-53最优）和标签格式（无变音符号文本最佳）；未来工作方向为提升数据集质量及开发音素感知模型以支持Tajweed敏感应用。

Abstract: Quran Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) aims to convert Quranic recitation into text, enabling applications such as aided memorisation tools and Quranic search engines. However, existing ASR models often exhibit high Word Error Rates (WER) on user-recited verses and lack full coverage of the Quranic corpus. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of domain-specific fine-tuning of pretrained Transformer-based models for Quranic ASR, using advanced speech feature extraction methods: Wav2Vec2.0, HuBERT, and XLS-R. These models apply self-supervised learning by masking portions of input audio and using Transformer architectures to learn context-aware speech features. The pretrained models are fine-tuned on a filtered Quranic dataset exceeding 870 hours of professional and user recitations. Through comprehensive ablation studies across feature extractors, output label formats, training strategies, and clip durations, we identify the key factors that affect transcription accuracy in this domain. Our best-performing configuration achieves a WER of 0.08 on the EveryAyah subset and 0.11 on the combined EveryAyah+Tarteel setting, representing roughly a five-percentage-point gain over the Citrinet baseline (WER = 0.163) while reducing combined-model training time from 140 hours to 40 hours. Arabic text without diacritics yields the best fine-tuning results, and Wav2Vec2-XLSR-53 provides the strongest overall representation. Future work includes improving dataset quality and developing phoneme-aware models to extract deeper speech feature representations for Tajweed-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [259] [Benchmarking Agentic Review Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19749)
*Dang Nguyen,Wanqing Hao,Yanai Elazar,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了AI辅助学术评审系统的有效性，测试了4个系统（2个开源、1个专有、1个基线）在6个LLM上的表现，通过论文质量追踪、错误检测基准和真实用户部署三个维度进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助研究的普及，传统同行评审系统面临压力，AI评审系统应运而生，但缺乏有效的评估框架来衡量这些系统的性能。

Method: 1）通过引用和接受决定等外部信号评估AI评审与论文质量的相关性；2）构建扰动基准测试，在8个arXiv学科类别的论文中注入四类错误，测量检测召回率；3）分析OpenAIReview的真实用户部署数据。

Result: 所有系统在成对准确率上均高于随机水平，最佳配置OpenAIReview+GPT-5.5达83.0%；错误检测方面，最强配置捕获71.6%注入错误，六模型联合召回率达83.3%；真实用户投票正面比例为1.44:1，主要投诉为误报和细节挑剔。

Conclusion: AI评审系统虽仍有改进空间，但已能有效追踪人类质量判断、捕获重要错误并获得用户正面反馈，多模型联合检测策略可进一步提升性能。

Abstract: A new class of agentic review systems are emerging as a remedy to the pressure placed on peer review systems by AI-assisted research, but it is unclear how they should be evaluated. We evaluate two open-source systems (OpenAIReview and coarse), one proprietary system (Reviewer3), and a zero-shot baseline, across six LLMs spanning frontier and efficient models. First, we study whether AI reviews on ICLR/NeurIPS papers track with papers' quality as approximated by external signals such as citations and acceptance decisions. Every system performs above chance in pairwise accuracy, and the best is OpenAIReview + GPT-5.5 at 83.0%. Second, to test whether systems can catch errors with known ground truth, we construct a perturbation benchmark that injects four categories of errors into papers across eight arXiv subject classes and measure detection recall. The strongest configuration (OpenAIReview + GPT-5.5) catches 71.6% of injected errors, leaving substantial room for improvement. The union of detections across six models reaches 83.3% recall, suggesting different models detect different errors and better harness design can potentially increase performance. Beyond these benchmarks, we study a public deployment of OpenAIReview with real users. Votes on its comments skew positive at 1.44 to 1, and the most common complaints are about false positives and minor nitpicks. Together, by evaluating full review systems backed by state-of-the-art models on real research papers, we show that while AI reviews still have room for improvement, they can already track human quality judgments well, catch important errors, and earn positive feedback from real users.

</details>


### [260] [Grounded Inference: Principles for Deterministically Encapsulated Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19753)
*Marty O'Neill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基础框架，定义四个AI混合架构原始元素和两个反模式，以降低生成模型整合到传统系统的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期采用者已意识到生成模型整合的风险，但领域仍缺乏基础框架来安全集成AI，导致高成本试错。

Method: 定义了四个AI混合架构的原始元素以实现概率模型的确定性封装，并识别了两个行业反模式作为警告。

Result: 框架提供了成功整合AI到传统系统的路径，并为生成模型提供商构建了下一代接口的基础。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅降低了集成风险，还为AI技术的未来发展铺平了道路。

Abstract: The incorporation of generative models into traditional computational systems presents both enormous opportunity and tremendous peril. Although many early adopters have realized these perils at great expense, the field still requires foundational frameworks to de-risk incorporation of AI into traditional systems. This manuscript establishes this foundation through the definition of four specific primitives of AI blended architecture, designed to enable deterministic encapsulation of probabilistic models. It further establishes two overarching anti-patterns broadly represented across industry to serve as warnings for engineers in this field. This framework was designed to enable successful integration of AI into traditional systems while providing a foundation upon which generative model providers could build the next generation of generative model interfaces.

</details>


### [261] [Optimal Scheduling in a Question-Answering Forum of Knowledge Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19759)
*Rohit Negi,Mustafa Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: As individuals turn to the Internet to find answers to questions they may have, several Question Answering (QA) forums have evolved, where users knowledgeable in certain topics can contribute their expertise to answering these requests for information. While these are currently volunteer based, we consider a future version employing knowledge workers who are experts in certain topics. In such a system, the request-answer processes forming the queuing system may utilize schedulers that assign requests in different topics to the experts in the forum, who may be able to answer them according to their expertise levels in different topics.
